Tickets and trace reports are in fabflows-tickets. An ACTIVE guard hook asks before package installs and blocks default-branch commits, destructive commands and credential-file access. It fails open. - **fairbuild-closeout**: Fairbuild Closeout: the closeout checklist for completed jobs against the documents and steps you require (final lien waivers, warranties, O&M manuals, as-builts, punch list, occupancy, final pay application and others you name): items missing, overdue, rejected or still open long after completion, and which jobs are ready for final billing. Drafts reminders to the project manager. Never submits a closeout package or a final invoice. ([fpavia111-creator/fairbuild-agents](https://github.com/fpavia111-creator/fairbuild-agents/tree/HEAD/packages/fairbuild-closeout) · 1 MCP config)
- **fairbuild-commitments**: Fairbuild Commitments: subcontracts and purchase orders against invoices and payments: committed cost still open, commitments billed past their value, unsigned subcontracts being billed, stale open POs, closed POs with a balance, retainage held at the wrong rate and invoices that name no commitment. Drafts vendor letters. Never closes or changes a PO. ([fpavia111-creator/fairbuild-agents](https://github.com/fpavia111-creator/fairbuild-agents/tree/HEAD/packages/fairbuild-commitments) · 1 MCP config)
- **fairbuild-delivery-tickets**: Fairbuild Delivery Tickets: supplier invoice lines for materials against the signed delivery tickets and your agreed prices: quantities billed past what was delivered, prices above the agreed price, extensions that do not add up, a ticket billed twice, lines with no ticket, and tickets not yet invoiced (to accrue). Drafts supplier credit requests. Never pays a supplier. ([fpavia111-creator/fairbuild-agents](https://github.com/fpavia111-creator/fairbuild-agents/tree/HEAD/packages/fairbuild-delivery-tickets) · 1 MCP config)
- **fairbuild-duplicate-vendors**: Fairbuild Duplicate Vendors: the vendor master and AP invoices for duplicates: vendors entered twice (same tax id, bank account, address, phone or name once legal forms are set aside), the same invoice entered under two numbers, two vendor records or twice for one vendor, duplicates already paid twice and duplicates still open. Drafts recovery letters. Never merges vendors, voids or pays. ([fpavia111-creator/fairbuild-agents](https://github.com/fpavia111-creator/fairbuild-agents/tree/HEAD/packages/fairbuild-duplicate-vendors) · 1 MCP config)
- **fairbuild-equipment-rentals**: Fairbuild Equipment Rentals: equipment rental invoices against the agreed day, week and month rates, the off-rent date and the hours the machine was used: billed above the cheapest rate combination, billed after it was called off, the same days billed twice, rentals sitting idle, and rentals with no job. Drafts credit requests. Never calls a rental off or pays. ([fpavia111-creator/fairbuild-agents](https://github.com/fpavia111-creator/fairbuild-agents/tree/HEAD/packages/fairbuild-equipment-rentals) · 1 MCP config)
- **fairbuild-insurance-certificates**: Fairbuild Insurance Certificates: subcontractor certificates of insurance against your requirements: required coverages missing, policies expired or expiring, limits below your minimums, missing additional insured, waiver of subrogation or primary and non-contributory wording, and policies that end before the sub's work does. Drafts certificate requests. Never contacts an insurer or blocks a vendor. ([fpavia111-creator/fairbuild-agents](https://github.com/fpavia111-creator/fairbuild-agents/tree/HEAD/packages/fairbuild-insurance-certificates) · 1 MCP config)
- **fairbuild-job-cash-flow**: Fairbuild Job Cash Flow: cash flow by job from receipts and disbursements: net cash position per job, jobs where you have paid out more than you collected, invoices to the owner unpaid past your collection days, and jobs negative two months running, with a month-by-month table. Drafts collection letters. Never moves money or changes a payment. ([fpavia111-creator/fairbuild-agents](https://github.com/fpavia111-creator/fairbuild-agents/tree/HEAD/packages/fairbuild-job-cash-flow) · 1 MCP config)
- **fairbuild-job-cost**: Fairbuild Job Cost: job cost against budget by cost code and cost type, from your budget and job cost exports (Procore, Sage, Foundation, Vista, QuickBooks). Flags lines over or projected over budget, spend running ahead of progress, unbudgeted codes and budget math errors, and drafts the cost review memo. Never changes a budget. ([fpavia111-creator/fairbuild-agents](https://github.com/fpavia111-creator/fairbuild-agents/tree/HEAD/packages/fairbuild-job-cost) · 1 MCP config)
- **fairbuild-lien-waivers**: Fairbuild Lien Waivers: every payment to a subcontractor or supplier matched to its lien waivers: payments made without a conditional waiver, unconditional waivers missing after the payment cleared, waivers for less than was paid, unsigned waivers, and final payments without final waivers. Drafts waiver requests. Never files or releases a lien and never pays. ([fpavia111-creator/fairbuild-agents](https://github.com/fpavia111-creator/fairbuild-agents/tree/HEAD/packages/fairbuild-lien-waivers) · 1 MCP config)
- **fairbuild-pay-applications**: Fairbuild Pay Applications: checks the arithmetic of a pay application in the G702/G703 layout before it goes out or before you approve a subcontractor's: each line's completed and stored total, percent and balance, retainage at your rates, lines billed past their scheduled value, work from previous applications that does not match the last one, and the summary page totals down to the payment due. Never submits or approves a pay application. ([fpavia111-creator/fairbuild-agents](https://github.com/fpavia111-creator/fairbuild-agents/tree/HEAD/packages/fairbuild-pay-applications) · 1 MCP config)
- **fairbuild-payment-release**: Fairbuild Payment Release: the release check before a subcontractor is paid: project manager approval, the owner paid for that month (pay-when-paid, if you use it), a conditional lien waiver for the invoice, a current insurance certificate, a W-9 on file and retainage withheld at the subcontract rate. Each invoice is cleared or held with its reasons, and payment releases are recorded only after one named person's click. Never pays. ([fpavia111-creator/fairbuild-agents](https://github.com/fpavia111-creator/fairbuild-agents/tree/HEAD/packages/fairbuild-payment-release) · 1 MCP config)
- **fairbuild-permits**: Fairbuild Permits: permits and inspections by job: permits expired or expiring, failed inspections with no re-inspection booked, inspections past their date with no result, inspections on permits you have no record of, and what is coming up. Writes a calendar file for permit expirations and inspections. Drafts notices to the project manager. Never applies to, renews with or schedules with a jurisdiction. ([fpavia111-creator/fairbuild-agents](https://github.com/fpavia111-creator/fairbuild-agents/tree/HEAD/packages/fairbuild-permits) · 1 MCP config)
- **fairbuild-punch-lists**: Fairbuild Punch Lists: punch list items by job, trade and subcontractor (Procore punch list exports recognised): items past due, open too long, reopened, with no one assigned, and open items on subcontractors you have already paid in full. Drafts punch notices to subcontractors. Never closes an item or charges a subcontractor. ([fpavia111-creator/fairbuild-agents](https://github.com/fpavia111-creator/fairbuild-agents/tree/HEAD/packages/fairbuild-punch-lists) · 1 MCP config)
- **fairbuild-retainage**: Fairbuild Retainage: retainage both ways: what owners hold from you by job and what you hold from subcontractors. Retainage held at the wrong rate or past a stop point, completed jobs whose retainage is due or overdue to bill, release billed but not collected, subcontractor retainage released before the owner paid yours, and retainage you now owe a subcontractor. Drafts release requests. Never bills, pays or releases. ([fpavia111-creator/fairbuild-agents](https://github.com/fpavia111-creator/fairbuild-agents/tree/HEAD/packages/fairbuild-retainage) · 1 MCP config)
- **fairbuild-sales-tax**: Fairbuild Sales Tax: tax charged on material invoices against the rates you supply for each job's jurisdiction: tax not charged (use tax to accrue), tax charged at another jurisdiction's rate, tax overcharged, tax charged on jobs you mark exempt, and deliveries to a different jurisdiction than the job. Drafts refund requests to vendors. Never files a return or posts an entry; not tax advice. ([fpavia111-creator/fairbuild-agents](https://github.com/fpavia111-creator/fairbuild-agents/tree/HEAD/packages/fairbuild-sales-tax) · 1 MCP config)
- **fairbuild-schedule-of-values**: Fairbuild Schedule of Values: the schedule of values line by line against what has been billed and what the field says is in place: lines billed ahead of progress (over billing), lines billed behind it (money not yet asked for), a schedule of values that does not add up to the contract, approved change orders missing from it, and billing on zero-value lines. Drafts the project manager memo. Never changes a billing. ([fpavia111-creator/fairbuild-agents](https://github.com/fpavia111-creator/fairbuild-agents/tree/HEAD/packages/fairbuild-schedule-of-values) · 1 MCP config)
- **fairbuild-service-tickets**: Fairbuild Service Tickets: service and trade tickets with technician timesheets (ServiceTitan layouts recognised): utilisation per technician from working against paid hours, revenue per paid hour, recall rate, completed tickets not invoiced and zero-dollar tickets. Technician names are masked in what the AI model sees. Drafts billing follow-ups. Never invoices a customer or runs payroll. ([fpavia111-creator/fairbuild-agents](https://github.com/fpavia111-creator/fairbuild-agents/tree/HEAD/packages/fairbuild-service-tickets) · 1 MCP config)
- **fairbuild-sub-compliance**: Fairbuild Sub Compliance: each active subcontractor's compliance documents against your list and the extra ones its trade needs (W-9, signed subcontract, licences, safety program, EMR letter and others you name): missing, expired, expiring, rejected, waiting too long for review, and safety ratings above your limit. Drafts document requests. Never approves or blocks a vendor. ([fpavia111-creator/fairbuild-agents](https://github.com/fpavia111-creator/fairbuild-agents/tree/HEAD/packages/fairbuild-sub-compliance) · 1 MCP config)
- **fairbuild-submittals**: Fairbuild Submittals: the submittal register worked back from the date material is needed on site: submittals not yet submitted past their latest submit date (required on site less lead time and review time), reviews running over the allowed days, revise-and-resubmit not resubmitted, third and later revisions, approvals that came after the order-by date, and spec sections with no submittal. Ages ball-in-court. Drafts reminders. Never approves or changes a submittal. ([fpavia111-creator/fairbuild-agents](https://github.com/fpavia111-creator/fairbuild-agents/tree/HEAD/packages/fairbuild-submittals) · 1 MCP config)
- **fairbuild-three-way-match**: Fairbuild Three-Way Match: three-way match for construction: each AP invoice against its subcontract or PO and the work or delivery your project manager approved: invoices with no commitment, a different vendor or job, cumulative billing past the commitment or past what was approved, no receipt or approval yet, and retainage not withheld. Holds or clears each invoice for review and records one-click payment approvals. Never pays. ([fpavia111-creator/fairbuild-agents](https://github.com/fpavia111-creator/fairbuild-agents/tree/HEAD/packages/fairbuild-three-way-match) · 1 MCP config)
- **fairbuild-timecards**: Fairbuild Timecards: field timecards against crew assignments, job status and the cost codes in each job budget: hours on a job the worker was not assigned to that day, on a closed job, or on a cost code the job has no budget for; days longer than your limit; weekend work; and entries made twice. Worker names are masked in what the AI model sees. Drafts recode requests. Never edits a timecard or runs payroll. ([fpavia111-creator/fairbuild-agents](https://github.com/fpavia111-creator/fairbuild-agents/tree/HEAD/packages/fairbuild-timecards) · 1 MCP config)
- **fairbuild-union-fringes**: Fairbuild Union Fringes: union fringe benefit remittances by local, fund and month against the hours your payroll shows and the contribution rates you supply: funds paid short or not at all, paid over, paid late, hours reported that do not match payroll, and hours for a local with no rates. Drafts fund letters and records one-click approvals to pay a shortfall. Never pays a fund. ([fpavia111-creator/fairbuild-agents](https://github.com/fpavia111-creator/fairbuild-agents/tree/HEAD/packages/fairbuild-union-fringes) · 1 MCP config)
- **fairbuild-warranty-callbacks**: Fairbuild Warranty Callbacks: warranty callbacks by job, trade and subcontractor: callbacks a subcontractor should pay for under its warranty, callbacks after your warranty ended that may be billable, the same problem coming back, callbacks open too long and callbacks with no one responsible, with cost by job and trade. Homeowner names and contacts are masked in what the AI model sees. Drafts subcontractor notices. Never charges anyone or contacts a homeowner. ([fpavia111-creator/fairbuild-agents](https://github.com/fpavia111-creator/fairbuild-agents/tree/HEAD/packages/fairbuild-warranty-callbacks) · 1 MCP config)
- **fairbuild-wip-schedule**: Fairbuild WIP Schedule: the work-in-progress schedule for month end by the cost-to-cost method: percent complete, earned revenue, over and under billings by job, the anticipated loss to recognise on loss jobs, cost past the estimate and jobs with no estimate. Drafts the over/under billing journal for review. Never posts to the ledger. ([fpavia111-creator/fairbuild-agents](https://github.com/fpavia111-creator/fairbuild-agents/tree/HEAD/packages/fairbuild-wip-schedule) · 1 MCP config)
- **fairclaim-anesthesia-claims**: Anesthesia claims priced and paired right: computes base, time, physical-status and qualifying-circumstance units, checks start and stop times, AA, QK, QY, QX, QZ and AD against how many rooms the anesthesiologist covered at once, pairs each CRNA claim with its medical direction, and flags overlaps. ([fpavia111-creator/fairclaim-agents](https://github.com/fpavia111-creator/fairclaim-agents/tree/HEAD/packages/fairclaim-anesthesia-claims) · 1 MCP config)
- **fairclaim-ar-aging**: Fairclaim AR Aging: ages open insurance balances by payer and bucket, warns before timely-filing limits pass, finds claims filed late or with no payer response, and builds the follow-up call queue by dollars and age. ([fpavia111-creator/fairclaim-agents](https://github.com/fpavia111-creator/fairclaim-agents/tree/HEAD/packages/fairclaim-ar-aging) · 1 MCP config)
- **fairclaim-charge-capture**: Matches completed visits to charges: finds visits with no charge, charges with no visit or on a cancelled one, late charge entry, missing expected codes, and E/M level mix by provider against peers. Patient identifiers are masked before results reach the model. ([fpavia111-creator/fairclaim-agents](https://github.com/fpavia111-creator/fairclaim-agents/tree/HEAD/packages/fairclaim-charge-capture) · 1 MCP config)
- **fairclaim-claim-rejections**: Reads 999 and 277CA acknowledgements, ties each syntax error to the claim and segment in the 837 you sent, lists front-end rejections with what to fix, and flags claims that were never acknowledged. ([fpavia111-creator/fairclaim-agents](https://github.com/fpavia111-creator/fairclaim-agents/tree/HEAD/packages/fairclaim-claim-rejections) · 1 MCP config)
- **fairclaim-claim-scrubber**: Scrubs 837 claims (or claim exports) before they go out, catching the errors payers reject on: NPIs, payer and member ids, dates, totals, codes, modifiers, diagnosis pointers, place of service, corrected-claim references and prior authorizations. ([fpavia111-creator/fairclaim-agents](https://github.com/fpavia111-creator/fairclaim-agents/tree/HEAD/packages/fairclaim-claim-scrubber) · 1 MCP config)
- **fairclaim-coding-audits**: Coding audits without the spreadsheet: draws a reproducible random sample of charges per provider, gives the auditor a worksheet, then scores the results (agree, upcoded, downcoded, wrong code, modifier or diagnosis error, not supported) with accuracy and dollar impact by provider. ([fpavia111-creator/fairclaim-agents](https://github.com/fpavia111-creator/fairclaim-agents/tree/HEAD/packages/fairclaim-coding-audits) · 1 MCP config)
- **fairclaim-credit-balances**: Works the credit balance report: tells patient credits from payer overpayments, counts days against each payer's refund deadline (60 days from identification for Medicare by default), finds credits that should go to an open balance first or are nearing unclaimed-property age, and drafts each refund for a person to approve. Patient identifiers are masked before results reach the model. ([fpavia111-creator/fairclaim-agents](https://github.com/fpavia111-creator/fairclaim-agents/tree/HEAD/packages/fairclaim-credit-balances) · 1 MCP config)
- **fairclaim-denial-appeals**: Drafts a first-level appeal for a denied claim with quotes from the payer policies you supply (file and line), tracks every appeal against its filing deadline and the payer's response time, and never sends anything itself. ([fpavia111-creator/fairclaim-agents](https://github.com/fpavia111-creator/fairclaim-agents/tree/HEAD/packages/fairclaim-denial-appeals) · 1 MCP config)
- **fairclaim-denial-worklist**: Sorts 835 denials by reason code and root cause into a worklist with the team and next step for each, learns payer-specific code meanings a person accepts, and never resubmits or appeals on its own. ([fpavia111-creator/fairclaim-agents](https://github.com/fpavia111-creator/fairclaim-agents/tree/HEAD/packages/fairclaim-denial-worklist) · 1 MCP config)
- **fairclaim-dental-claims**: Dental claims that go out right: checks tooth numbers, surfaces against the restoration code, anterior and posterior codes, quadrants for scaling and root planing, frequency limits against the patient's history, extracted teeth, crown replacement windows and the attachments payers ask for, from an 837D or a practice export. ([fpavia111-creator/fairclaim-agents](https://github.com/fpavia111-creator/fairclaim-agents/tree/HEAD/packages/fairclaim-dental-claims) · 1 MCP config)
- **fairclaim-dme-claims**: DME claims and rentals kept straight: checks rental and purchase modifiers, the capped-rental month (KH, KI, KJ) and the 13-month cap, oxygen months, right and left on paired items, prior authorization, the order before delivery and units, and tracks every rental to its next modifier. ([fpavia111-creator/fairclaim-agents](https://github.com/fpavia111-creator/fairclaim-agents/tree/HEAD/packages/fairclaim-dme-claims) · 1 MCP config)
- **fairclaim-eligibility-checks**: Reads 271 eligibility responses against the appointment list and flags visits with inactive coverage, member not found, plan ending before the visit, another payer primary or no response, with copay and deductible to collect. ([fpavia111-creator/fairclaim-agents](https://github.com/fpavia111-creator/fairclaim-agents/tree/HEAD/packages/fairclaim-eligibility-checks) · 1 MCP config)
- **fairclaim-fee-schedule**: Prices each code on your fee schedule from the Medicare RVU and GPCI files (RVU x GPCI x conversion factor), finds fees below what payers would allow (money left on the table when payers pay the lesser of charge or allowed), below your target percent of Medicare, or with no Medicare price, and drafts a fee update for a person to approve. ([fpavia111-creator/fairclaim-agents](https://github.com/fpavia111-creator/fairclaim-agents/tree/HEAD/packages/fairclaim-fee-schedule) · 1 MCP config)
- **fairclaim-good-faith-estimates**: Keeps good faith estimates for self-pay and uninsured patients on time: counts business days from scheduling or request to each estimate's due date, finds missing and late estimates, compares final bills with estimates per provider against the 400-dollar dispute threshold, and drafts an estimate for a person to send. Patient identifiers are masked before results reach the model. ([fpavia111-creator/fairclaim-agents](https://github.com/fpavia111-creator/fairclaim-agents/tree/HEAD/packages/fairclaim-good-faith-estimates) · 1 MCP config)
- **fairclaim-lab-claims**: Lab claims that get paid: checks the CLIA number and certificate (QW on waived tests, nothing outside a waiver), finds panel components billed one by one or with their panel, venipuncture more than once a day, repeats without 91, diagnoses that do not support a test, and tests ordered more often than your rules allow. ([fpavia111-creator/fairclaim-agents](https://github.com/fpavia111-creator/fairclaim-agents/tree/HEAD/packages/fairclaim-lab-claims) · 1 MCP config)
- **fairclaim-modifier-checks**: Checks the modifiers on each claim line before it goes out: missing 25 on an E/M with a procedure, 25 or 57 on the wrong code, conflicting pairs, 59 with X modifiers, missing laterality, bilateral units, repeat modifiers with no first service, pricing order, and payer rules. Patient identifiers are masked before results reach the model. ([fpavia111-creator/fairclaim-agents](https://github.com/fpavia111-creator/fairclaim-agents/tree/HEAD/packages/fairclaim-modifier-checks) · 1 MCP config)
- **fairclaim-ncci-edits**: Checks claims against the CMS NCCI procedure-to-procedure edits and medically unlikely edits files you download, by date of service, with the modifier indicator, bypass modifiers and MUE adjudication indicator, plus payer pair edits your team adds. ([fpavia111-creator/fairclaim-agents](https://github.com/fpavia111-creator/fairclaim-agents/tree/HEAD/packages/fairclaim-ncci-edits) · 1 MCP config)
- **fairclaim-patient-registration**: Checks patient registration and insurance records before the visit: member id shape per payer and Medicare MBI format, missing or impossible birth dates, subscriber and guarantor gaps, ended coverage, the same plan entered twice, and likely duplicate charts. Patient identifiers are masked before results reach the model. ([fpavia111-creator/fairclaim-agents](https://github.com/fpavia111-creator/fairclaim-agents/tree/HEAD/packages/fairclaim-patient-registration) · 1 MCP config)
- **fairclaim-patient-statements**: Plans each patient statement cycle: who gets a first, second or final statement, who is held (insurance still pending, payment plan, dispute, estate or bankruptcy, bad address, small balance, recent payment) and who reaches collections review, and drafts a plain-language statement for a person to send. Patient identifiers are masked before results reach the model. ([fpavia111-creator/fairclaim-agents](https://github.com/fpavia111-creator/fairclaim-agents/tree/HEAD/packages/fairclaim-patient-statements) · 1 MCP config)
- **fairclaim-payer-contract-rates**: Compares what each payer pays per code against Medicare and each other, weighted by your volume, flags rates below your floor, keeps a calendar of contract end dates and notice deadlines (auto-renewal traps), and drafts a rate request for a person to send. ([fpavia111-creator/fairclaim-agents](https://github.com/fpavia111-creator/fairclaim-agents/tree/HEAD/packages/fairclaim-payer-contract-rates) · 1 MCP config)
- **fairclaim-payer-scorecards**: Scores each payer from your 835s and claim submissions: days to pay, denial rate and top denial reasons, share of billed paid, reversals, and a month-by-month trend, against your targets. Patient identifiers are masked before results reach the model. ([fpavia111-creator/fairclaim-agents](https://github.com/fpavia111-creator/fairclaim-agents/tree/HEAD/packages/fairclaim-payer-scorecards) · 1 MCP config)
- **fairclaim-payment-plans**: Tracks patient payment plans against your policy: installments due after a grace period, missed payments and plans to review for default, plans paid off or ahead, monthly amounts and terms outside your policy, plan options for a new balance, and a draft plan agreement for a person to send. Patient identifiers are masked before results reach the model. ([fpavia111-creator/fairclaim-agents](https://github.com/fpavia111-creator/fairclaim-agents/tree/HEAD/packages/fairclaim-payment-plans) · 1 MCP config)
- **fairclaim-practice-go-live**: Go-live readiness for a new practice: checks the billing NPI, tax ID and billing address, the provider roster, every payer's EDI, ERA, EFT and credentialing enrollment, payer IDs and the fee schedule against the charges you will bill, and lists the blockers before go-live. ([fpavia111-creator/fairclaim-agents](https://github.com/fpavia111-creator/fairclaim-agents/tree/HEAD/packages/fairclaim-practice-go-live) · 1 MCP config)
- **fairclaim-price-transparency**: Hospital price transparency without the fines: checks a machine-readable file against the CMS v3.0 tall CSV template (headers, attestation, code pairs, the twelve conditional rules), compares it with the chargemaster, and computes the median, 10th and 90th percentile allowed amounts and counts from 835s the CMS way. ([fpavia111-creator/fairclaim-agents](https://github.com/fpavia111-creator/fairclaim-agents/tree/HEAD/packages/fairclaim-price-transparency) · 1 MCP config)
- **fairclaim-prior-authorizations**: Matches claims or scheduled services to your prior-authorization log and 278 responses, and flags services with no authorization, expired or not-yet-valid dates, codes not on the authorization, units used up, pended or denied reviews, and authorizations about to run out. ([fpavia111-creator/fairclaim-agents](https://github.com/fpavia111-creator/fairclaim-agents/tree/HEAD/packages/fairclaim-prior-authorizations) · 1 MCP config)
- **fairclaim-provider-credentialing**: Keeps provider credentialing and payer enrollment on track: license, DEA, board and malpractice expirations, CAQH re-attestation every 120 days, NPI check digits, enrollments pending too long, denied or not yet effective, and revalidation dates, with a work list and a calendar (.ics) of every due date. ([fpavia111-creator/fairclaim-agents](https://github.com/fpavia111-creator/fairclaim-agents/tree/HEAD/packages/fairclaim-provider-credentialing) · 1 MCP config)
- **fairclaim-remit-deposits**: Matches 835 remittances to bank deposits by trace number, then amount and date, explains provider-level adjustments (recoveries, interest, levies), and drafts the payer inquiry for money without a remittance or a remittance without money. ([fpavia111-creator/fairclaim-agents](https://github.com/fpavia111-creator/fairclaim-agents/tree/HEAD/packages/fairclaim-remit-deposits) · 1 MCP config)
- **fairclaim-revenue-kpis**: Revenue cycle KPIs from your own claims and remittances: denial rate, first-pass rate, net collection, days to pay, days in A/R and unanswered claims, by payer, month and provider, against the targets you set. ([fpavia111-creator/fairclaim-agents](https://github.com/fpavia111-creator/fairclaim-agents/tree/HEAD/packages/fairclaim-revenue-kpis) · 1 MCP config)
- **fairclaim-telehealth-claims**: Telehealth claims coded the way each payer wants: checks place of service 02 or 10 against where the patient was, the 95, GT, 93 or FQ modifiers by payer, audio-only against video codes, deleted phone codes, the provider's license in the patient's state, consent for virtual check-ins, and same-day in-person visits. ([fpavia111-creator/fairclaim-agents](https://github.com/fpavia111-creator/fairclaim-agents/tree/HEAD/packages/fairclaim-telehealth-claims) · 1 MCP config)
- **fairclaim-underpayments**: Compares what payers allowed on each 835 line with your contract (fee schedule, percent of Medicare or percent of charge, with modifier and multiple-procedure rules) and drafts the reprocessing request for a person to send. ([fpavia111-creator/fairclaim-agents](https://github.com/fpavia111-creator/fairclaim-agents/tree/HEAD/packages/fairclaim-underpayments) · 1 MCP config)
- **fairdb-operations-kit**: Complete operations kit for FairDB PostgreSQL as a Service - VPS setup, PostgreSQL management, customer provisioning, monitoring, and backup automation ([jeremylongshore/tons-of-skills-marketplace](https://github.com/jeremylongshore/tons-of-skills-marketplace/tree/HEAD/plugins/devops/fairdb-operations-kit) · ★2.9k · 1 skill, 4 commands, 1 agent)

- **fairhaul-accessorial-charges**: Fairhaul Accessorial Charges: finds where your accessorial charges come from (liftgate, residential, appointment, redelivery, detention and more) by location, carrier and lane, flags charges nobody ordered and charges above your contract, and drafts the address-book fixes that stop the repeat surprises. Never changes your TMS. ([fpavia111-creator/fairhaul-agents](https://github.com/fpavia111-creator/fairhaul-agents/tree/HEAD/packages/fairhaul-accessorial-charges) · 1 MCP config)
- **fairhaul-air-freight-audit**: Fairhaul Air Freight Audit: audits air freight invoices: chargeable weight from actual and volumetric weight, the rate for the right weight break, the minimum charge, fuel and security surcharges per kilo, and other charges your rate sheet does not allow; drafts disputes to the forwarder. Never pays or sends. ([fpavia111-creator/fairhaul-agents](https://github.com/fpavia111-creator/fairhaul-agents/tree/HEAD/packages/fairhaul-air-freight-audit) · 1 MCP config)
- **fairhaul-broker-settlements**: For freight brokers: settles every carrier payment against its rate confirmation, approved accessorials, quick-pay terms and notice of assignment, finds overpayments, double payments and payments to the wrong party, and drafts the next payment batch for a person to release. ([fpavia111-creator/fairhaul-agents](https://github.com/fpavia111-creator/fairhaul-agents/tree/HEAD/packages/fairhaul-broker-settlements) · 1 MCP config)
- **fairhaul-carrier-scorecards**: Fairhaul Carrier Scorecards: scores each carrier on on-time pickup and delivery, tender acceptance, billing accuracy, claims, tracking and cost per mile from your own shipment history, with your weights, ranks them and drafts the quarterly review note. Never sends it. ([fpavia111-creator/fairhaul-agents](https://github.com/fpavia111-creator/fairhaul-agents/tree/HEAD/packages/fairhaul-carrier-scorecards) · 1 MCP config)
- **fairhaul-carrier-vetting**: Checks your carrier master and booked loads for warning signs of fraud and double brokering: shared phones, emails or bank accounts, new authority, lapsed insurance, recent remit-to changes. It flags; a person verifies and decides. ([fpavia111-creator/fairhaul-agents](https://github.com/fpavia111-creator/fairhaul-agents/tree/HEAD/packages/fairhaul-carrier-vetting) · 1 MCP config)
- **fairhaul-carton-sizing**: Fairhaul Carton Sizing: checks every parcel's carton against what was inside, finds the smallest carton in your catalog that fits, works out the dimensional weight and freight you would save, flags packages heading for oversize charges, and drafts the carton set and packing rule changes. Never changes your WMS. ([fpavia111-creator/fairhaul-agents](https://github.com/fpavia111-creator/fairhaul-agents/tree/HEAD/packages/fairhaul-carton-sizing) · 1 MCP config)
- **fairhaul-container-demurrage**: Fairhaul Container Demurrage: tracks every import container's free time at the port and with the line, counts the days left before demurrage and detention start, prices what is accruing from your tariff tiers, checks the line's demurrage and detention invoices and drafts disputes. Never pays or books anything. ([fpavia111-creator/fairhaul-agents](https://github.com/fpavia111-creator/fairhaul-agents/tree/HEAD/packages/fairhaul-container-demurrage) · 1 MCP config)
- **fairhaul-contract-rerating**: Fairhaul Contract Rerating: re-rates your shipment history under every carrier contract you hold (linehaul, minimums, discounts, fuel and accessorials), shows what each carrier would have cost, the cheapest carrier by lane and what you left on the table, and drafts routing guide changes. Never changes your routing guide. ([fpavia111-creator/fairhaul-agents](https://github.com/fpavia111-creator/fairhaul-agents/tree/HEAD/packages/fairhaul-contract-rerating) · 1 MCP config)
- **fairhaul-customs-entries**: Checks your customs broker's entry lines against your commercial invoices: value, quantity, origin, duty arithmetic and consistency of codes and rates across entries, with the days left to correct each entry. It gives no classification advice and files nothing. ([fpavia111-creator/fairhaul-agents](https://github.com/fpavia111-creator/fairhaul-agents/tree/HEAD/packages/fairhaul-customs-entries) · 1 MCP config)
- **fairhaul-detention-and-lumper**: Fairhaul Detention and Lumper: checks detention, layover and lumper charges against your dock times, appointments, free time and receipts, ranks facilities by dwell and detention cost, and drafts disputes. Never pays and never sends. ([fpavia111-creator/fairhaul-agents](https://github.com/fpavia111-creator/fairhaul-agents/tree/HEAD/packages/fairhaul-detention-and-lumper) · 1 MCP config)
- **fairhaul-dock-scheduling**: Plans a day of dock appointments across your doors, crews and hours, lists requests that do not fit, and reviews past appointments for no-shows, late arrivals, waits and how long each kind of load really takes. It never books or confirms an appointment. ([fpavia111-creator/fairhaul-agents](https://github.com/fpavia111-creator/fairhaul-agents/tree/HEAD/packages/fairhaul-dock-scheduling) · 1 MCP config)
- **fairhaul-duplicate-payments**: Fairhaul Duplicate Payments: searches years of freight payments for invoices paid twice: the same invoice under another number format, the same PRO billed twice, a carrier paid under two vendor names, and same-amount look-alikes, nets off credits already received, and drafts recovery requests. Never moves money. ([fpavia111-creator/fairhaul-agents](https://github.com/fpavia111-creator/fairhaul-agents/tree/HEAD/packages/fairhaul-duplicate-payments) · 1 MCP config)
- **fairhaul-edi-files**: Fairhaul EDI Files: reads X12 transport files (204 load tenders, 210 invoices, 214 status, 990 responses, 997/999 acknowledgements), checks envelopes and counts, converts them to spreadsheets, and reconciles invoices against tenders, status and acknowledgements. Never transmits EDI. ([fpavia111-creator/fairhaul-agents](https://github.com/fpavia111-creator/fairhaul-agents/tree/HEAD/packages/fairhaul-edi-files) · 1 MCP config)
- **fairhaul-freight-accruals**: Fairhaul Freight Accruals: builds the month-end freight accrual from your shipments and carrier invoices: shipped but not billed, estimated from the quote, your contract or lane history, by cost center, with a draft journal entry and an accuracy check on last month. Never posts to your ledger. ([fpavia111-creator/fairhaul-agents](https://github.com/fpavia111-creator/fairhaul-agents/tree/HEAD/packages/fairhaul-freight-accruals) · 1 MCP config)
- **fairhaul-freight-allocation**: Fairhaul Freight Allocation: splits each freight bill across the orders, customers, SKUs and cost centers on the shipment by weight, volume, value, pallets or units, gives freight cost per unit by SKU and customer, and drafts the allocation journal for review. Never posts to your ledger. ([fpavia111-creator/fairhaul-agents](https://github.com/fpavia111-creator/fairhaul-agents/tree/HEAD/packages/fairhaul-freight-allocation) · 1 MCP config)
- **fairhaul-freight-bill-audit**: Fairhaul Freight Bill Audit: audits truckload and LTL freight bills (CSV, Excel or X12 210) against your own contract rates, fuel table and accessorial terms, finds duplicates and math errors, and drafts carrier disputes. Never pays and never sends. ([fpavia111-creator/fairhaul-agents](https://github.com/fpavia111-creator/fairhaul-agents/tree/HEAD/packages/fairhaul-freight-bill-audit) · 1 MCP config)
- **fairhaul-freight-claims**: Fairhaul Freight Claims: finds loss, shortage and damage on delivered shipments, works out the claim amount and the filing deadline from your terms, tracks open claims for carrier acknowledgement, payment and short payment, and drafts claim letters. Never files a claim itself. ([fpavia111-creator/fairhaul-agents](https://github.com/fpavia111-creator/fairhaul-agents/tree/HEAD/packages/fairhaul-freight-claims) · 1 MCP config)
- **fairhaul-freight-emissions**: Estimates the greenhouse gas emissions of your shipments from weight, distance and mode, using emission factors you choose, with totals by mode, carrier, lane and customer, a data quality score and labelled mode-shift what-ifs. Every figure is an estimate; nothing is submitted anywhere. ([fpavia111-creator/fairhaul-agents](https://github.com/fpavia111-creator/fairhaul-agents/tree/HEAD/packages/fairhaul-freight-emissions) · 1 MCP config)
- **fairhaul-freight-margins**: Compares the freight you charged each customer with what the freight actually cost: recovery by customer and freight terms, orders shipped free that should not have been, collect orders you paid for, and how a different free-shipping threshold would have changed the result. ([fpavia111-creator/fairhaul-agents](https://github.com/fpavia111-creator/fairhaul-agents/tree/HEAD/packages/fairhaul-freight-margins) · 1 MCP config)
- **fairhaul-fuel-surcharges**: Fairhaul Fuel Surcharges: checks every fuel surcharge against your carriers' fuel tables and the diesel index for the week each load moved, spots carriers billing on the wrong week, compares fuel tables side by side and shows what a diesel move would cost you. Never pays or sends. ([fpavia111-creator/fairhaul-agents](https://github.com/fpavia111-creator/fairhaul-agents/tree/HEAD/packages/fairhaul-fuel-surcharges) · 1 MCP config)
- **fairhaul-lane-spend**: Fairhaul Lane Spend: maps your freight spend by lane and mode, tracks cost per hundredweight and per mile month by month, finds LTL shipments on the same lane and day that could ride together and heavy LTL that should be quoted as truckload, and drafts the consolidation plan for your shipping team. Never changes orders or bookings. ([fpavia111-creator/fairhaul-agents](https://github.com/fpavia111-creator/fairhaul-agents/tree/HEAD/packages/fairhaul-lane-spend) · 1 MCP config)
- **fairhaul-load-tracking**: Fairhaul Load Tracking: reads your open loads and their tracking events, finds loads with stale or no tracking, missed pickups and ETAs that run past the delivery appointment, ranks carriers on tracking compliance and drafts the delay notice to your customer. Never messages anyone itself. ([fpavia111-creator/fairhaul-agents](https://github.com/fpavia111-creator/fairhaul-agents/tree/HEAD/packages/fairhaul-load-tracking) · 1 MCP config)
- **fairhaul-ltl-reweighs**: Fairhaul LTL Reweighs: checks LTL weight and class corrections against your declared dimensions, the density scale and the inspection certificate, separates the corrections worth challenging from the ones that mean your own product data is wrong, and drafts the disputes and item-master fixes. Never pays or sends. ([fpavia111-creator/fairhaul-agents](https://github.com/fpavia111-creator/fairhaul-agents/tree/HEAD/packages/fairhaul-ltl-reweighs) · 1 MCP config)
- **fairhaul-ocean-freight-audit**: Fairhaul Ocean Freight Audit: audits ocean freight invoices line by line against your service contract: base rate per container by port pair, BAF, peak season and other surcharges with their validity dates, charges the contract does not allow, duplicates and container counts, and drafts disputes to the line or forwarder. Never pays or sends. ([fpavia111-creator/fairhaul-agents](https://github.com/fpavia111-creator/fairhaul-agents/tree/HEAD/packages/fairhaul-ocean-freight-audit) · 1 MCP config)
- **fairhaul-parcel-refunds**: Fairhaul Parcel Refunds: audits UPS, FedEx, DHL and USPS invoice data for late deliveries, duplicate tracking numbers, voided labels billed, residential charges on business addresses, repeat address corrections and dimensional-weight overbilling, with claim-by dates and refund request drafts. Never files claims itself. ([fpavia111-creator/fairhaul-agents](https://github.com/fpavia111-creator/fairhaul-agents/tree/HEAD/packages/fairhaul-parcel-refunds) · 1 MCP config)
- **fairhaul-proof-of-delivery**: Fairhaul Proof of Delivery: matches every delivered or invoiced shipment to its proof of delivery, flags missing, unsigned, short or exception-noted PODs and delivery dates that disagree with the invoice, holds the matching invoices for review, and drafts POD requests to carriers. Never releases or holds a payment itself. ([fpavia111-creator/fairhaul-agents](https://github.com/fpavia111-creator/fairhaul-agents/tree/HEAD/packages/fairhaul-proof-of-delivery) · 1 MCP config)
- **fairhaul-returns-tracking**: Follows every return from authorisation to credit: return labels never used, returns received but not credited, carriers charging return freight twice, and which customers, SKUs and reasons cost the most freight to bring back. ([fpavia111-creator/fairhaul-agents](https://github.com/fpavia111-creator/fairhaul-agents/tree/HEAD/packages/fairhaul-returns-tracking) · 1 MCP config)
- **fairhaul-rfp-bid-scoring**: Fairhaul RFP Bid Scoring: scores carrier bids from a freight RFP lane by lane against your incumbent rates and volumes, builds award scenarios with your rules (share caps, incumbent preference, backup carriers, capacity), shows the savings and the lanes nobody covered, and drafts award letters. Never loads rates or sends awards. ([fpavia111-creator/fairhaul-agents](https://github.com/fpavia111-creator/fairhaul-agents/tree/HEAD/packages/fairhaul-rfp-bid-scoring) · 1 MCP config)
- **fairhaul-spot-quotes**: Fairhaul Spot Quotes: lines up the spot quotes you collected for each load on one all-in basis (adding fuel and accessorials a quote leaves out), drops expired quotes and ones that miss the delivery date, compares them with what the lane usually costs you, and drafts the booking confirmation. Never books a load itself. ([fpavia111-creator/fairhaul-agents](https://github.com/fpavia111-creator/fairhaul-agents/tree/HEAD/packages/fairhaul-spot-quotes) · 1 MCP config)
- **fairhaul-standard-freight-cost**: Compares the standard freight built into item costs with what freight actually cost per unit, finds the SKUs and lanes where the standard is stale, and drafts new standards for a person to load. ([fpavia111-creator/fairhaul-agents](https://github.com/fpavia111-creator/fairhaul-agents/tree/HEAD/packages/fairhaul-standard-freight-cost) · 1 MCP config)
- **fairlease-1099-filings**: Gets vendor and owner 1099s ready: totals each payee's payments for the tax year against the threshold you give, leaves out card payments, flags missing or malformed tax IDs, missing addresses, shared tax IDs and payees not in the directory, and drafts W-9 requests. Tax IDs are always masked. ([fpavia111-creator/fairlease-agents](https://github.com/fpavia111-creator/fairlease-agents/tree/HEAD/packages/fairlease-1099-filings) · 1 MCP config)
- **fairlease-acquisition-diligence**: Reconciles a seller's rent roll with the trailing-twelve-month income statement before you buy or lend: rent roll against rent collected, occupancy, expired leases still billed, months missing or out of line, and NOI. ([fpavia111-creator/fairlease-agents](https://github.com/fpavia111-creator/fairlease-agents/tree/HEAD/packages/fairlease-acquisition-diligence) · 1 MCP config)
- **fairlease-affordable-recerts**: Tracks affordable housing recertification dates against the schedule in your policy file: due dates, the notices your policy sends before each one, overdue and soon-due households, and households missing from the tracker or the rent roll; drafts reminder notices. It never decides eligibility or changes rent. ([fpavia111-creator/fairlease-agents](https://github.com/fpavia111-creator/fairlease-agents/tree/HEAD/packages/fairlease-affordable-recerts) · 1 MCP config)
- **fairlease-budget-variance**: Compares actuals with the budget by account for the month and year to date, flags the variances your management agreement says must be explained, unbudgeted spend and the year-end forecast, and drafts the owner's variance memo. ([fpavia111-creator/fairlease-agents](https://github.com/fpavia111-creator/fairlease-agents/tree/HEAD/packages/fairlease-budget-variance) · 1 MCP config)
- **fairlease-cam-reconciliation**: Reconciles common area maintenance and operating expenses for commercial leases: expense pools, pro-rata shares, gross-ups, caps, base years, admin fees and tenant estimates, with statement drafts. ([fpavia111-creator/fairlease-agents](https://github.com/fpavia111-creator/fairlease-agents/tree/HEAD/packages/fairlease-cam-reconciliation) · 1 MCP config)
- **fairlease-capital-projects**: Tracks capital projects against budget, commitments, schedule and reserve balances, flags overruns, late and stalled projects, and drafts budget change requests for owners. ([fpavia111-creator/fairlease-agents](https://github.com/fpavia111-creator/fairlease-agents/tree/HEAD/packages/fairlease-capital-projects) · 1 MCP config)
- **fairlease-delinquency-worklist**: Turns an aging report or tenant ledger into a delinquency worklist with stages from your own policy, late-fee checks and reminder drafts. Never serves notices or files evictions. ([fpavia111-creator/fairlease-agents](https://github.com/fpavia111-creator/fairlease-agents/tree/HEAD/packages/fairlease-delinquency-worklist) · 1 MCP config)
- **fairlease-estoppel-review**: Checks returned tenant estoppel certificates against your lease abstracts before a sale or refinancing (rent, dates, deposit, area, options, amendments, landlord defaults claimed), tracks which are missing or stale, and drafts follow-up requests. ([fpavia111-creator/fairlease-agents](https://github.com/fpavia111-creator/fairlease-agents/tree/HEAD/packages/fairlease-estoppel-review) · 1 MCP config)
- **fairlease-hoa-accounts**: Checks HOA and condo association owner accounts against your assessment and collection policy (missing or wrong assessments, late fees charged or missed, days delinquent and the next step your policy names) and tracks violations against your notice, cure and hearing steps; drafts owner notices. ([fpavia111-creator/fairlease-agents](https://github.com/fpavia111-creator/fairlease-agents/tree/HEAD/packages/fairlease-hoa-accounts) · 1 MCP config)
- **fairlease-lease-abstracts**: Abstracts commercial lease text into the Fairlease leases format with a citation for every term, and checks existing abstracts against the lease text. ([fpavia111-creator/fairlease-agents](https://github.com/fpavia111-creator/fairlease-agents/tree/HEAD/packages/fairlease-lease-abstracts) · 1 MCP config)
- **fairlease-lease-renewals**: Lists leases coming up for renewal with offer deadlines, proposes rents within your own increase cap, checks offers already made and drafts renewal offer letters. ([fpavia111-creator/fairlease-agents](https://github.com/fpavia111-creator/fairlease-agents/tree/HEAD/packages/fairlease-lease-renewals) · 1 MCP config)
- **fairlease-leasing-funnel**: Checks the leasing funnel from guest cards and lead exports: leads not answered or answered late, follow-ups due, approved applicants with no lease, stale and duplicate leads, and conversion and cost per lease by source; drafts follow-up messages for a person to send. ([fpavia111-creator/fairlease-agents](https://github.com/fpavia111-creator/fairlease-agents/tree/HEAD/packages/fairlease-leasing-funnel) · 1 MCP config)
- **fairlease-listing-checks**: Checks rental listings across your website and listing sites against the rent roll: vacant or soon-vacant units not listed, units listed while occupied, rent, deposit and unit details that differ from the rent roll or between sites, stale dates and duplicates, and listing wording for a person to review; drafts correction requests. ([fpavia111-creator/fairlease-agents](https://github.com/fpavia111-creator/fairlease-agents/tree/HEAD/packages/fairlease-listing-checks) · 1 MCP config)
- **fairlease-management-fees**: Recomputes the management, leasing, renewal and markup fees you charged each owner from the terms of their management agreement, and flags overcharges, undercharges, duplicates and fees the agreement does not provide for. ([fpavia111-creator/fairlease-agents](https://github.com/fpavia111-creator/fairlease-agents/tree/HEAD/packages/fairlease-management-fees) · 1 MCP config)
- **fairlease-occupancy-and-vacancy**: Reads a rent roll and shows occupancy, leased percentage, exposure, vacancy loss and loss to lease unit by unit, with long-vacant units and rents far from market flagged. ([fpavia111-creator/fairlease-agents](https://github.com/fpavia111-creator/fairlease-agents/tree/HEAD/packages/fairlease-occupancy-and-vacancy) · 1 MCP config)
- **fairlease-owner-statements**: Checks owner statements before they go out: the math, beginning against last month's ending, reserves held, distributions made below the reserve, negative balances, and totals against the owner ledger; drafts contribution requests. ([fpavia111-creator/fairlease-agents](https://github.com/fpavia111-creator/fairlease-agents/tree/HEAD/packages/fairlease-owner-statements) · 1 MCP config)
- **fairlease-percentage-rent**: Works out percentage rent from tenant sales reports (natural or fixed breakpoints, exclusions, partial years), flags late and missing reports and drafts the tenant statement. ([fpavia111-creator/fairlease-agents](https://github.com/fpavia111-creator/fairlease-agents/tree/HEAD/packages/fairlease-percentage-rent) · 1 MCP config)
- **fairlease-pms-migration**: Checks a property management system migration by comparing the old system's rent roll with the new one's at cutover: units missing or added, tenants, rents, deposits, balances, areas and lease dates that changed, and totals that do not tie; drafts a punch list for the implementation team. Go-live is a person's call. ([fpavia111-creator/fairlease-agents](https://github.com/fpavia111-creator/fairlease-agents/tree/HEAD/packages/fairlease-pms-migration) · 1 MCP config)
- **fairlease-property-taxes**: Tracks property tax assessments, installments and the appeal deadlines you enter, flags large assessment increases, unpaid or short installments, and writes a calendar of due dates. ([fpavia111-creator/fairlease-agents](https://github.com/fpavia111-creator/fairlease-agents/tree/HEAD/packages/fairlease-property-taxes) · 1 MCP config)
- **fairlease-rent-escalations**: Checks rent billed against each lease's escalation schedule (fixed steps, percentages, amounts and CPI with floors and caps) and lists the increases coming up. ([fpavia111-creator/fairlease-agents](https://github.com/fpavia111-creator/fairlease-agents/tree/HEAD/packages/fairlease-rent-escalations) · 1 MCP config)
- **fairlease-rent-roll-audit**: Fairlease Rent Roll Audit: checks a rent roll against your lease abstracts: rents, escalations, dates, deposits, areas, tenants, vacancies and holdovers, unit by unit. ([fpavia111-creator/fairlease-agents](https://github.com/fpavia111-creator/fairlease-agents/tree/HEAD/packages/fairlease-rent-roll-audit) · 1 MCP config)
- **fairlease-security-deposits**: Checks every move-out deposit against your own rules (deadline, itemisation, wear and tear, useful-life proration) and drafts the itemised deposit statement. Refunds are a person's job. ([fpavia111-creator/fairlease-agents](https://github.com/fpavia111-creator/fairlease-agents/tree/HEAD/packages/fairlease-security-deposits) · 1 MCP config)
- **fairlease-short-term-rental-payouts**: Reconciles short-term rental payouts from Airbnb, Vrbo and other channels against your reservations and bank deposits: missing, short or duplicate payouts, channel fees and cleaning fees off your rates, nights that do not match, and payouts that never reached the bank; drafts payout inquiries. ([fpavia111-creator/fairlease-agents](https://github.com/fpavia111-creator/fairlease-agents/tree/HEAD/packages/fairlease-short-term-rental-payouts) · 1 MCP config)
- **fairlease-tenant-insurance**: Checks every tenant's insurance certificate against the limits, additional insured and dates their lease requires, and drafts requests for updated certificates. ([fpavia111-creator/fairlease-agents](https://github.com/fpavia111-creator/fairlease-agents/tree/HEAD/packages/fairlease-tenant-insurance) · 1 MCP config)
- **fairlease-trust-reconciliation**: Three-way reconciliation of a trust or escrow bank account: bank statement against the book register against the owner and tenant ledgers, with outstanding checks, deposits in transit, unrecorded bank items and negative ledgers. ([fpavia111-creator/fairlease-agents](https://github.com/fpavia111-creator/fairlease-agents/tree/HEAD/packages/fairlease-trust-reconciliation) · 1 MCP config)
- **fairlease-unit-inspections**: Compares move-out inspections with move-in item by item, flags units overdue for their periodic inspection and poor items with no work order, and drafts inspection notices. ([fpavia111-creator/fairlease-agents](https://github.com/fpavia111-creator/fairlease-agents/tree/HEAD/packages/fairlease-unit-inspections) · 1 MCP config)
- **fairlease-unit-turns**: Tracks unit turns from move-out to make-ready to move-in: days against your target, cost by trade against your budget, tenant-chargeable items and the rent lost while the unit sits. ([fpavia111-creator/fairlease-agents](https://github.com/fpavia111-creator/fairlease-agents/tree/HEAD/packages/fairlease-unit-turns) · 1 MCP config)
- **fairlease-utility-billback**: Re-works RUBS utility billbacks from the bills and occupancy, by your own method, and finds over-recovery, vacant units billed, bills that spike, and charges that differ from the split. ([fpavia111-creator/fairlease-agents](https://github.com/fpavia111-creator/fairlease-agents/tree/HEAD/packages/fairlease-utility-billback) · 1 MCP config)
- **fairlease-vendor-compliance**: Checks every vendor's liability and workers' comp insurance, licence and tax form against your own requirements, finds payments made while a vendor was out of compliance, and drafts certificate requests. ([fpavia111-creator/fairlease-agents](https://github.com/fpavia111-creator/fairlease-agents/tree/HEAD/packages/fairlease-vendor-compliance) · 1 MCP config)
- **fairlease-work-orders**: Fairlease Work Orders: ages open work orders against your own targets by priority, finds repeat problems in the same unit, likely emergencies filed as routine, slow vendors and cost outliers. ([fpavia111-creator/fairlease-agents](https://github.com/fpavia111-creator/fairlease-agents/tree/HEAD/packages/fairlease-work-orders) · 1 MCP config)
- **fairmake-bom-cost-rollup**: Fairmake BOM Cost Rollup: every assembly's cost rebuilt from its bill of materials and latest purchase prices, against its standard cost, with missing and stale prices and BOM loops. Loads nothing. ([fpavia111-creator/fairmake-agents](https://github.com/fpavia111-creator/fairmake-agents/tree/HEAD/packages/fairmake-bom-cost-rollup) · 1 MCP config)
- **fairmake-bom-routing-gaps**: Fairmake BOM Routing Gaps: made items with no BOM or routing, inactive or unknown components, zero quantities, routing steps with no work center or time, and BOM loops. Edits nothing. ([fpavia111-creator/fairmake-agents](https://github.com/fpavia111-creator/fairmake-agents/tree/HEAD/packages/fairmake-bom-routing-gaps) · 1 MCP config)
- **fairmake-calibration-due**: Fairmake Calibration Due: gauges overdue or due soon, never calibrated, and work orders measured with a gauge that was overdue or had failed calibration, with a due-date calendar. - **fairmind-integration**: Team collaboration with 6 role-based agents and 9 technology-specific skills. Agents: Atlas (Tech Lead), Echo (Software Engineer), Tess (QA Engineer), Code Reviewer, Debug Detective, Shield (Cybersecurity). Skills cover frontend, backend, Python, LangChain, Playwright, and AI systems. ([FairMind-Gen-AI-Studio/fairmind-integration](https://github.com/FairMind-Gen-AI-Studio/fairmind-integration) · 9 skills, 20 commands, 12 agents, 1 hook file)
- **fairprice**: Estimate what a stock is worth by writing down what has to be true for today's price to make sense. Picks between sum-of-the-parts, normalized earnings times one multiple, and finite-life NPV, then reports a bear/base/bull range with falsifiable checkpoints. ([wiwihaun/claude-skills](https://github.com/wiwihaun/claude-skills/tree/HEAD/plugins/fairprice) · 1 skill)
- **fairshop-3pl-invoices**: Audits a 3PL invoice line by line against your rate card, shipped orders, receipts and pallet counts: rates above the card, orders billed twice, picks for orders that never shipped, more units received or pallets stored than counted, and charges not on the card. Drafts the invoice dispute; never pays. ([fpavia111-creator/fairshop-agents](https://github.com/fpavia111-creator/fairshop-agents/tree/HEAD/packages/fairshop-3pl-invoices) · 1 MCP config)
- **fairshop-ad-spend**: Reads ad platform exports (Amazon Ads, Google Ads, Meta) against your orders and stock: campaigns under your target return, spend with no sales, campaigns over budget, ads for SKUs that are out of stock, and platforms that claim more sales than the SKU sold. Drafts a campaign review; never pauses a campaign or changes a budget. ([fpavia111-creator/fairshop-agents](https://github.com/fpavia111-creator/fairshop-agents/tree/HEAD/packages/fairshop-ad-spend) · 1 MCP config)
- **fairshop-affiliate-commissions**: Checks affiliate commission statements against your orders and terms: commission on refunded or cancelled orders, orders not found, rates above your terms, sale amounts above the order, the same order paid twice, and affiliates buying through their own link. Drafts the reversal request; never approves a payout. ([fpavia111-creator/fairshop-agents](https://github.com/fpavia111-creator/fairshop-agents/tree/HEAD/packages/fairshop-affiliate-commissions) · 1 MCP config)
- **fairshop-carrier-adjustments**: Checks carrier adjustments (UPS, FedEx, USPS) against the weights and dimensions you entered on the label: adjustments your own data disproves, with a dispute-by date, adjustments that are right because your product data is wrong (and the SKUs that keep causing them), address corrections, and adjustments past their window. Drafts the dispute; never files it or pays. ([fpavia111-creator/fairshop-agents](https://github.com/fpavia111-creator/fairshop-agents/tree/HEAD/packages/fairshop-carrier-adjustments) · 1 MCP config)
- **fairshop-cash-over-short**: Reads register closeouts and bank deposits for physical stores: drawers over or short beyond your tolerance, deposits missing or different from the counted cash, and cashiers with repeated shortages (names masked). Drafts a review note for the store manager; never deducts pay or moves money. ([fpavia111-creator/fairshop-agents](https://github.com/fpavia111-creator/fairshop-agents/tree/HEAD/packages/fairshop-cash-over-short) · 1 MCP config)
- **fairshop-coupon-abuse**: Checks every order that used a discount code against your code rules: single-use and limited codes used too often, codes used outside their dates or under the minimum order, new-customer codes used by returning shoppers, staff codes used outside staff, and codes you never issued. Drafts a code review; never disables a code or cancels an order. ([fpavia111-creator/fairshop-agents](https://github.com/fpavia111-creator/fairshop-agents/tree/HEAD/packages/fairshop-coupon-abuse) · 1 MCP config)
- **fairshop-duplicate-listings**: Finds duplicate and orphan listings across channels: the same SKU listed twice on one channel (and at different prices), near-identical titles under different SKUs, one GTIN shared by several SKUs, and listings for SKUs not in your catalog. Drafts a clean-up list; never ends or merges a listing. ([fpavia111-creator/fairshop-agents](https://github.com/fpavia111-creator/fairshop-agents/tree/HEAD/packages/fairshop-duplicate-listings) · 1 MCP config)
- **fairshop-fba-reimbursements**: Finds Amazon FBA reimbursements owed: units lost or damaged in Amazon's warehouses and not found or reimbursed, refunds with no customer return, and reimbursements below your unit value, each with a claim-by date. Drafts the case text; a person files it in Seller Central. ([fpavia111-creator/fairshop-agents](https://github.com/fpavia111-creator/fairshop-agents/tree/HEAD/packages/fairshop-fba-reimbursements) · 1 MCP config)
- **fairshop-fba-storage-fees**: Checks Amazon FBA storage fees against the storage rates you confirm, warns before inventory lots cross into the next aged-inventory bracket, lists lots already paying an aged surcharge, and SKUs whose storage costs more than their margin. Drafts a removal or sell-through plan; never creates removal orders. ([fpavia111-creator/fairshop-agents](https://github.com/fpavia111-creator/fairshop-agents/tree/HEAD/packages/fairshop-fba-storage-fees) · 1 MCP config)
- **fairshop-gift-cards**: Works out gift card and store credit liability from card and activity exports: balances that do not match the ledger, cards spent below zero, large manual issues with no order, redemptions before issue, and dormant cards, by age. - **fairshop-inventory-listings**: Compares stock by location (warehouse, 3PL, FBA) with the quantities listed on each channel: listings above what their locations can ship, listings at zero while stock sits on the shelf, inactive listings with stock, listings for SKUs you do not stock, and stock listed nowhere. - **fairshop-landed-cost**: Spreads freight, duty, broker and other import costs over the SKUs of each shipment (by weight or value, as you set) to get a landed unit cost, compares it with the cost recorded in your store, and lists shipments with no costs and costs with no shipment. - **fairshop-map-pricing**: Checks advertised prices from your own channels and from resellers against your minimum advertised price (MAP) list: your own listings below MAP first, resellers below it (and repeat ones), authorised exceptions, and SKUs not on the list. - **fairshop-marketplace-claims**: Lines up Amazon A-to-z, eBay, Walmart and Etsy claims with your shipment and delivery records: respond-by dates, which claims your tracking can defend, which need a signature or photos, which are likely refunds, and which are overdue. - **fairshop-marketplace-fees**: Checks every marketplace fee line (Amazon, eBay, Etsy, Walmart) against the fee schedule you confirm: fees above the schedule, fee types not in it, percent fees with no sale amount, and fee rates that changed month to month. - **fairshop-stockouts**: Reads daily stock snapshots and orders per SKU: orders taken while out of stock, days out of stock and an estimate of the sales lost, and SKUs that will run out before a reorder could arrive, with a suggested quantity.
